▶ the Strands AgentExecutor satisfies the same semantic contract
  ✔ one model tool use becomes a semantic operation call, before any native execution (9.510792ms)

  ✔ a denial is reported to the model as faithfully as a success (16.050333ms)
  ✔ several tool uses in one turn are captured together (3.640792ms)
  ✔ cancellation propagates where the framework supports it (1.431125ms)
  ✔ no callback in this package can reach the gateway, a dispatcher, or a settlement path (11.329208ms)
  ✔ the bridge forwards the projected observation and shapes no semantics of its own (6.812333ms)
  ✔ the model bridge builds its specs from the projection, not from the framework registry (0.733ms)
  ✔ driven by the real AgentController, no operation reaches an executor without the Harness (21.684833ms)

✔ imported MCP identity is chosen locally (6.947709ms)

✔ remote metadata is information, never permission (68.4825ms)
